# META
~~~ini
description=SysML 8.3.18.8 validateTransitionFeatureMembershipGuardExpression applies to a conditional succession, because GuardedSuccession and GuardedTargetSuccession are TransitionUsages: its guard is judged like a transition guard, by folded value or result type, in both the `first ... if ... then` and the inline `if ... then` spellings
specification=OMG SysML 2.0 Language (formal/26-03-02)
specification_url=https://www.omg.org/spec/SysML/2.0/Language/PDF
validation_rule=8.3.18.8 validateTransitionFeatureMembershipGuardExpression
source_expectation=accepted

expectation=diagnostics
rule_id=sysml-2.0:8.3.18.8:validateTransitionFeatureMembershipGuardExpression
coverage_role=secondary
libraries=standard

~~~
# SOURCE
~~~sysml
package Guards {
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    part def Controller {
        attribute count : Integer;
        attribute ready : Boolean;
        attribute anything;
        action def Flow {
            action a1;
            action a2;
            action a3;

            // Conforming: a Boolean feature, a comparison, a logical operator.
            first a1 if ready then a2;
            first a1 if count > 0 then a2;
            first a1 if ready and count > 0 then a2;

            // Undecided, not reported: an untyped referent.
            first a1 if anything then a2;

            // Invalid: a constant, an Integer feature, and Integer arithmetic.
            first a2 if 42 then a3;
            first a2 if count then a3;
            first a2 if count + 1 then a3;
        }
        action def Inline {
            action a1;
            action a2;
            action a3;
            action a4;
            first start;
            then a1;
            if ready then a2;
            then a2;
            if count then a3;
            else a4;
        }
    }
}
~~~
